**[00:13:00.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000262)

**[00:13:02.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000263)

**[00:13:04.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000264)

**[00:13:06.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000265)

**[00:13:08.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000266)

**[00:13:10.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000267)

**[00:13:12.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000268)

**[00:13:14.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000269)

**[00:13:16.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000270)

**[00:13:19.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000271)  
Last year po,

**[00:13:20.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000272)  
as manifested by SDS

**[00:13:22.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000273)  
Hernandez, there was

**[00:13:24.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000274)  
that P255 billion

**[00:13:26.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000275)  
peso, essentially

**[00:13:28.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000276)  
amount that was taken out

**[00:13:30.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000277)  
from DPWH flood control

**[00:13:32.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000278)  
projects that had to be

**[00:13:33.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000279)

**[00:13:36.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000280)  
So, it was clear that

**[00:13:38.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000281)  
there was that amount

**[00:13:40.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000282)  
that we could distribute across

**[00:13:42.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000283)  
the agencies. Ngayon po,

**[00:13:44.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000284)  
the BARSC is very cognizant

**[00:13:46.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000285)  
that over the course of the deliberations,

**[00:13:48.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000286)  
sobrang dami pong mga programa

**[00:13:50.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000287)  
na nabanggit

**[00:13:51.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000288)  
ng ating mga kasamahan, ng ating

**[00:13:54.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000289)  
mga ahensya,

**[00:13:56.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000290)  
na sobrang kailangan mapondohan

**[00:13:57.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000291)  
na walang pondo sa NEP

**[00:13:59.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000292)  
o di kaya hindi sapat yung

**[00:14:01.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000293)  
pondo sa National Expenditure Program.

**[00:14:03.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000294)  
Halimbawa po, magbabanggit na po siguro

**[00:14:05.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000295)  
ako ng ilan, under the

**[00:14:07.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000296)  
Department of Education, it has been

**[00:14:09.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000297)  
manifested that the AREL program

**[00:14:12.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000298)  
has no budget. It has zero

**[00:14:13.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000299)  
budget in the NEP. That is something

**[00:14:16.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000300)  
that we cannot ignore.

**[00:14:18.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000301)  
It was also mentioned that

**[00:14:19.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000302)  
the importance of

**[00:14:22.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000303)  
the school's security,

**[00:14:24.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000304)  
the funding for school security,

**[00:14:26.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000305)  
which was also not there in the NEP,

**[00:14:27.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000306)  
it needs to be funded. Service

**[00:14:29.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000307)  
contracting, for example, fuel

**[00:14:31.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000308)  
transport subsidy,

**[00:14:34.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000309)  
and all of these

**[00:14:36.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000310)  
very, very important

**[00:14:38.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000311)  
programs

**[00:14:40.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000312)  
that were either not funded

**[00:14:42.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000313)  
or underfunded in the NEP,

**[00:14:44.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000314)  
nabanggit po yan over the course

**[00:14:46.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000315)  
of our months of deliberation.

**[00:14:48.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000316)  
So sa parte naman po

**[00:14:49.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000317)  
ng BARSC, narinig po

**[00:14:52.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000318)  
namin yun at nakita po namin

**[00:14:54.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000319)  
na kailangan nating pondohan,

**[00:14:56.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000320)  
so dahil wala pong

**[00:14:57.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000321)  
P255 billion, katulad ng last year

**[00:15:00.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000322)  
na madaling hugutin,

**[00:15:01.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000323)  
kailangan po talaga namin suriin

**[00:15:03.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000324)  
kung papaano po nating mapupondohan

**[00:15:05.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000325)  
ito pong mga programa na ito.

**[00:15:07.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000326)  
The budget is a zero-sum game.

**[00:15:10.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000327)  
If you fund one program,

**[00:15:11.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000328)  
you have to take from another

**[00:15:13.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000329)  
program. So that makes the job

**[00:15:15.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000330)  
of the BARSC doubly hard

**[00:15:17.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000331)  
this year. So thank you so much po

**[00:15:19.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000332)  
for manifesting that.

**[00:18:57.680] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000409)

**[00:19:06.020] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000410)

**[00:19:08.840] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000411)

**[00:19:12.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000412)

**[00:19:14.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-000413)

**[00:19:29.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000414)  
Thank you very much po, Mayno.

**[00:19:33.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000415)  
I'm just looking through my notes.

**[00:19:35.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000416)  
I'm just looking at all the mandatory appropriations.

**[00:19:38.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000417)  
But on top of my head po, in theory po, tulad po na nabanggit,

**[00:19:43.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000418)  
ni Mayno, tumaas po yung budget natin from 6.8 trillion up to 7.2 trillion pesos.

**[00:19:50.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000419)  
So in theory, dapat po may 400 billion na kayagdagan na pwede nating ma-allocate.

**[00:19:56.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000420)  
Kasa lang po, marami pong mga mandatory allocations.

**[00:20:02.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000421)  
Essentially, the whole 400 million went to these mandatory allocations.

**[00:20:07.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000422)  
For example po, I don't know if I'll remember everything now,

**[00:20:11.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000423)  
but a huge part of it,

**[00:20:13.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000424)  
went to the increase in salary standardization law,

**[00:20:21.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000425)  
the new tranche of the SSL.

**[00:20:24.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000426)  
Mayroon din pong napunta doon po sa increase in subsistence allowance under the MUP.

**[00:20:29.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000427)  
Mayroon din po sa interest payments.

**[00:20:32.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000428)  
Mayroon din pong, for example, under DA, there's this new law called ANSEF.

**[00:20:38.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000429)  
20 billion went to that.

**[00:20:40.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000430)  
There's also the increase in national tax allocation.

**[00:20:43.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000431)  
I think that's malaki po.

**[00:20:47.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000432)  
And then there's also this new law for the judiciary,

**[00:20:50.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000433)  
the fiscal autonomy law.

**[00:20:52.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000434)  
So essentially, 12 billion went towards that.

**[00:20:56.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000435)  
So ito pong, hindi ko po ma-breakdown yung kabuuan ng 400 billion,

**[00:21:02.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000436)  
kung saan po napunta exactly,

**[00:21:04.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000437)  
pero napunta po yun sa mandatory allocation.

**[00:21:09.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000438)  
So kung susumahin po natin,

**[00:21:12.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000439)  
at inuneto natin, wala po tayong increase

**[00:21:15.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000440)  
doon po sa talagang programmed appropriations

**[00:21:18.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000441)  
na atin pong pwedeng magalaw o di kaya maikot

**[00:21:24.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000442)  
doon po sa loob ng HGAB or yung House General Appropriations Bill.

**[00:21:31.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000443)  
Yes po.

**[00:21:33.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000444)  
Representative Brian po?

**[00:21:34.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000445)  
So siguro po, on that note, Madam Chair,

**[00:21:37.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000446)  
since we're talking about an increased budget to 7 trillion,

**[00:21:40.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000447)  
konting paalala sa mga kasama natin dito sa BARSC,

**[00:21:45.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000448)  
lahat tayo magpapropose ng amendments,

**[00:21:48.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000449)  
but all of these amendments, ultimately,

**[00:21:51.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000450)  
all this money comes from our taxpayers

**[00:21:53.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000451)  
who this year have struggled so much,

**[00:21:56.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000452)  
yung middle class po natin,

**[00:21:58.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000453)  
ang mga small to medium enterprises,

**[00:22:00.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000454)  
to provide these taxes for our program.

**[00:22:02.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000455)  
So ang priority po natin sana are low-cost

**[00:22:06.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000456)  
but high-impact amendments per agency.

**[00:22:10.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000458)  
So yun lang po.

**[00:22:12.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000459)  
On behalf of the MSMEs and middle class workers,

**[00:22:16.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000460)  
konting paalala, let's try to be as efficient as possible

**[00:22:20.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-000461)  
with our amendments, Madam Chair.

**[00:22:22.240] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-000462)

**[00:22:48.140] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-000473)

**[00:22:55.180] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-000476)

**[00:23:13.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000484)

**[00:23:18.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000486)

**[00:23:35.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000492)

**[00:23:40.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000494)

**[00:23:43.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000495)  
So you'll hear special provisions

**[00:23:45.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000496)

**[00:28:10.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000597)

**[00:28:32.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000605)

para po tumugon, um,

**[00:28:39.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000608)  
sa mga pangangailangan ng

**[00:28:40.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000609)

**[00:35:05.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000759)

**[00:36:44.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000780)

**[00:36:54.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000781)

**[00:36:59.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000782)

**[00:37:05.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000783)

**[00:37:09.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000784)

**[00:37:14.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000785)

**[00:37:23.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000786)

**[00:37:24.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000787)

**[00:37:31.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000788)

**[00:50:41.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001002)

**[00:50:42.260] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001003)  
We are very much aware of how EDCOM had already reached a very important role

**[00:50:50.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001004)  
in terms of legislation, education, funding, and accountability.

**[00:50:56.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001005)  
And while the education problems cut across many sectors and committees,

**[00:51:00.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001006)  
it's often fragmented and vulnerable,

**[00:51:03.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001007)  
especially when it comes to changes in political leadership and congressional priorities.

**[00:51:08.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001008)  
Hence, the intention of...

**[00:51:12.260] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001009)  
of CAPE, which will serve as a cross-committee and cross-party platform where shared education reform agenda can be discussed will also translate to evidence and recommendations from EDCOM 2 and other education stakeholders, including oversight initiatives, budget priorities, and sustained policy action.

**[00:51:33.720] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001010)  
Ultimately, the case for supporting CAPE rests on a simple principle, and that is education reform requires more than good policies.

**[00:51:41.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001011)  
It requires a durable coalition capable of carrying those policies from evidence to legislation, from legislation to funding, from funding to implementation, and accountability.

**[00:51:54.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001012)  
With this, Madam Chair, allow me to put forward a motion

**[00:51:59.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001013)  
to

**[00:52:02.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001014)  
include under the UP system,

**[00:52:10.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001015)  
particularly the UP system,

**[00:52:11.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001016)  
the UP Center for Education and Development of UP Diliman,

**[00:52:15.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001017)  
an increase in the amount of 70 million

**[00:52:17.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001018)  
to cover the program related to EDCOM 2 and CAPE.

**[00:52:24.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001019)  
So, moved, Madam Chair.

**[00:52:26.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001020)  
There is a motion. Any second?

**[00:52:28.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001021)

**[00:52:30.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001022)

**[00:52:32.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001023)  
There being no objection,

**[00:52:34.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001024)  
it has been duly seconded. The motion is carried.

**[00:52:36.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001025)  
70 million pesos for the UP Center for Education and Development

**[00:52:40.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001026)  
under the UP system.

**[00:52:41.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001027)

**[00:52:44.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001030)  
Representative Brian.

**[00:53:11.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001035)

**[00:53:11.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001036)

**[00:53:23.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001039)

**[00:53:30.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001040)

**[00:53:34.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001041)  
So, last year, the BARSC and the House, and eventually,

**[00:53:41.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001042)  
both chambers, the House and the Senate, gave an additional 1 billion pesos for Project NOAA

**[00:53:47.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001043)  
in the 2026 budget.

**[00:53:51.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001044)  
Binigay po natin 'yun para po mas mapaganda po ng Project NOAH 'yung kanilang hazard mapping

**[00:53:57.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001045)  
at makatulong po din sila sa pag-vet ng mga proyekto patungkol po sa flood control

**[00:54:04.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001046)  
at iba pang mga flood management designs and mechanisms.

**[00:54:08.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001047)  
So, I believe I had

**[00:54:11.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001048)  
a conversation with Professor Mahar Lagmay and President Jijil Jimenez of the UP system,

**[00:54:16.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001049)  
and they've already received the 1 billion pesos.

**[00:54:19.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001050)  
The SARO for the 1 billion pesos has already been released to the UP system,

**[00:54:24.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001051)  
and now, Project NOAA is in the process of ramping up their operation.

**[00:54:29.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001052)  
So, ang lagi pong sinasabi ngayon ni Professor Mahar,

**[00:54:32.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001053)  
kahit daw po bahay ng manok, makikita na daw po doon sa satellite mapping ng Project NOAA

**[00:54:37.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001054)  
kasi maging 1 by 1 square meter na daw po yung mapping.

**[00:54:41.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001055)  
And I'm also very happy to report that we are currently working with Project NOAA

**[00:54:50.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001056)  
and the UP Resilience Institute, as well as other stakeholders in the scientific community.

**[00:54:57.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001057)

**[00:55:03.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001058)  
all the items under 002 in DPWH, so flood control,

**[00:55:11.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001059)

**[00:55:12.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001060)

**[00:55:18.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001061)

**[00:55:25.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001062)

**[00:55:30.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001063)

**[00:55:37.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001064)  
at sana po mas mapaganda pa nila

**[00:55:41.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001065)  
'yung nasimulan nila dito po.

**[00:55:42.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001066)

**[00:55:50.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001067)

**[00:55:56.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001068)

**[01:06:52.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001272)

**[01:07:51.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001281)

**[01:07:52.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001282)

**[01:07:58.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001283)

**[01:08:02.760] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001284)

**[01:08:05.120] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001285)  
Madam Chair, I could not...

**[01:08:06.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001286)

**[01:08:07.960] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001287)  
Yes, thank you. My apologies Madam Chair.

**[01:08:09.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001288)  
It's okay po. I'm the Chairperson of the Committee on Agriculture and Food,

**[01:08:13.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001289)  
Representative Mark Enverga.

**[01:08:14.540] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001290)  
Thank you once again Madam Chair.

**[01:08:15.900] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001291)  
And again, I wish to re-emphasize the...

**[01:08:18.900] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001292)  
the looming problem of El Nino,

**[01:08:23.220] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001293)  
as well as the rising costs of fuel,

**[01:08:25.380] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001294)  
which would impact the agricultural sector as well.

**[01:08:30.720] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001295)  
So, Madam Chair, for the record,

**[01:08:33.860] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001296)  
I have the copy of the requests of the Department of Agriculture.

**[01:08:38.660] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001297)  
And it is quite lengthy,

**[01:08:41.160] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001298)  
but unfortunately we should focus,

**[01:08:42.760] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001299)  
I agree with you Madam Chair,

**[01:08:44.200] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001300)  
that we should re-focus our attentions in the mitigation projects

**[01:08:47.760] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001301)  
and programs that would help somehow mitigate the impacts of El Nino.

**[01:08:54.820] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001302)  
If I may start Madam Chair,

**[01:08:57.100] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001303)  
currently the Department of Agriculture already has a fertilizer assistance program.

**[01:09:03.480] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001304)  
So, they propose an expanded fertilizer assistance program,

**[01:09:08.260] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001305)  
currently with a NEP budget in the year 2027

**[01:09:11.280] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001306)  
of 14,443,499,000.

**[01:09:17.760] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001307)  
May I move Madam Chair,

**[01:09:20.480] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001308)  
that since this program is very much needed,

**[01:09:22.920] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001309)  
so that we keep the planting intentions of our farmers to be there,

**[01:09:26.520] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001310)  
and to assure them that there is support from government,

**[01:09:30.120] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001311)  
particularly for fertilizer.

**[01:09:32.080] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001312)  
May I move Madam Chair,

**[01:09:33.500] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001313)  
that I propose the amount of increase of 500 million pesos

**[01:09:39.580] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001314)  
to the expanded fertilizer assistance program of DA.

**[01:09:43.460] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001315)  
So, I move Madam Chair.

**[01:09:45.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001316)  
There is a motion to add...

**[01:09:47.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001317)  
an additional half a billion pesos

**[01:09:50.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001318)  
to the expanded fertilizer assistance.

**[01:09:53.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001319)  
It has been duly seconded.

**[01:09:55.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001320)  
There being no objection, the motion is carried.

**[01:09:58.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001321)  
I remember this, Chair Mark,

**[01:10:00.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001322)  
nung nandoon po tayo sa budget deliberation ng DA,

**[01:10:03.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001323)  
we asked Secretary Kiko Tiu Laurel

**[01:10:05.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001324)

**[01:10:12.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001325)  
Ito po yung pinakauna talaga na binanggit nila sa Kiko.

**[01:10:15.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001326)  
And I believe, even during the uplift,

**[01:10:17.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001327)  
ito po yung una nila ding pinondohan

**[01:10:20.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001328)  
kasi ito yung unang nagmamahal

**[01:10:21.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001329)  
kapag tumataas po yung presyo ng crudo.

**[01:10:25.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001330)  
Yung fertilizer po talaga sobrang nagmamahal.

**[01:10:28.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001331)  
So, thank you, Chair Mark, for manifesting that.

**[01:10:32.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001332)  
Madam Chair, before we continue...

**[01:10:34.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001333)  
Representative Brian.

**[01:10:35.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001334)  
Madam Chair, before we continue,

**[01:10:37.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001335)  
because the budget for fertilizer is unspecified per crop,

**[01:10:42.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001336)  
there is a specific crop that I would be remiss in not championing.

**[01:10:47.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001337)  
I hope that we can align some of this budget

**[01:10:51.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001338)  
to support the Ube industry of the Philippines.

**[01:10:54.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001339)  
I believe there are requests within the department

**[01:10:57.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001340)  
to support the Ube industry.

**[01:10:59.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001341)  
And I think that sayang naman

**[01:11:01.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001342)  
kung maiiwan tayo ulit ng neighbors natin pagdating sa ube.

**[01:11:07.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001343)  
This is something nationally important to us.

**[01:11:11.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001344)  
So, if I may suggest and if I may move

**[01:11:14.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001345)  
that we realign 100 million of that,

**[01:11:17.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001346)  
to support specifically Ube farming here in the Philippines.

**[01:11:30.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001347)  
May I defer to Chair Mark?

**[01:11:35.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001348)  
Because I don't want to preempt,

**[01:11:36.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001349)  
because perhaps I want to give the floor to Chair Mark Iverka.

**[01:11:41.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001350)

**[01:11:46.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001351)  
Yes.

**[01:11:47.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001352)  
Maybe rather than realigning,

**[01:11:50.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001353)  
I don't know if the body would like to add a separate amount for Ube.

**[01:11:55.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001354)  
So, Chair Mark, I'd like to defer to you.

**[01:11:57.880] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001355)  
Yes. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:11:59.200] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001356)

**[01:12:06.640] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001357)

**[01:12:10.720] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001358)  
to include Ube export,

**[01:12:16.620] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001359)  
ano, with the

**[01:12:17.500] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001360)  
in terms of the budget.

**[01:12:23.800] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001362)  
Yes.

**[01:12:24.020] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001363)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:12:28.660] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001364)  
May I continue?

**[01:12:29.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001365)  
Yes.

**[01:12:30.160] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001366)  
So, yes, I would accede to Donable Brian Poe.

**[01:12:33.640] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001367)  
Probably, Madam Chair, would it be okay at the proper time

**[01:12:36.460] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001368)  
when we get to the item with the indulgence of Donable Brian Poe?

**[01:12:40.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001369)  
Sure.

**[01:12:40.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001370)  
For now, Madam Chair, I'll retract my motion.

**[01:12:43.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001371)

**[01:12:50.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001373)

**[01:12:52.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001374)

**[01:13:10.540] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001386)

**[01:13:10.960] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001387)

**[01:13:17.500] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001390)

**[01:13:25.020] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001391)  
Madam Chair, again, in line of the problem with the El Nino phenomenon,

**[01:13:29.080] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001392)  
the super El Nino phenomenon,

**[01:13:31.140] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001393)  
and, of course, the looming probable increases in gas prices,

**[01:13:37.540] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001394)  
I also propose a program that was not included in the NEP for 2027,

**[01:13:43.540] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001395)  
which is the solar pumps for irrigation.

**[01:13:47.380] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001396)

**[01:13:47.500] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001397)  
If I may clarify, Madam Chair, this is not included under the National Irrigation Administration.

**[01:13:52.680] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001398)

**[01:13:59.000] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001399)  
So, this would fall under the DABSWM.

**[01:14:04.460] Rep. Wilfrido Mark M. Enverga** (asr-001400)

**[01:31:12.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001801)

**[01:31:15.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001802)  
So, thank you po for reminding me.

**[01:31:17.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001803)  
Yung isa po natin na gustong gawin din

**[01:31:20.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001804)  
para po siguraduhin na maging Super El Nino ready

**[01:31:23.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001805)  
yung budget po natin para sa taong 2027

**[01:31:27.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001806)  
ay malagyan po ng pondo yung presidential assistance

**[01:31:30.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001807)  
to farmers and fisher folk.

**[01:31:33.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001808)  
I was very surprised po when I saw the NEP

**[01:31:37.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001809)  
na zero po yung budget ng presidential assistance

**[01:31:40.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001810)  
to farmers and fisher folk or PAF.

**[01:31:43.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001811)  
Kasi alam naman po natin,

**[01:31:44.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001812)  
nung nagkaroon po, nung nagsimula po yung Middle East crisis

**[01:31:48.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001813)  
nito po mga nakaraang buwan,

**[01:31:50.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001814)  
yun po talaga yung naging unang inilabas

**[01:31:53.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001815)  
para po tumugon sa pangangailangan na ating mga magsasaka

**[01:31:56.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001816)  
dahil nga po ang laki nung tinaas nung presyo ng crudo

**[01:31:59.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001817)  
kaya po naapektuhan natin.

**[01:32:00.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001818)  
Nakaapektuhan din po yung presyo ng fertilizers

**[01:32:03.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001819)  
at iba pang mga kemikal at iba pang mga farm inputs.

**[01:32:06.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001820)  
So I was really surprised when I saw that

**[01:32:09.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001821)  
PAF had zero budget in the NEP.

**[01:32:13.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001822)  
So last year po, tayo po bilang BARSC din

**[01:32:17.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001823)  
and of course upon the...

**[01:32:23.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001824)

**[01:32:24.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001825)  
Bojie Dy. Talaga pong inilaban po

**[01:32:27.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001826)  
to ni Speaker last year.

**[01:32:30.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001827)

**[01:32:31.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001828)  
tayo ng 10 billion pesos

**[01:32:33.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001829)  
para sa Presidential Assistance to

**[01:32:35.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001830)  
Farmers and Fisher Folk. So

**[01:32:36.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001831)

**[01:32:39.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001832)

**[01:32:41.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001833)

**[01:32:42.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001834)

**[01:32:44.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001835)

**[01:32:46.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001836)

**[01:32:49.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001837)

**[01:32:51.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001838)

**[01:32:53.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001839)

**[01:32:55.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001840)

**[01:32:57.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001841)

**[01:32:59.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001842)

**[01:33:01.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001843)

**[01:33:59.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001872)

**[02:21:44.240] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002994)

**[02:21:46.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002995)

**[02:21:48.800] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002996)

**[02:21:50.360] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002997)

**[02:21:54.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002998)  
and the Ukraine-Russia war,

**[02:21:57.720] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002999)  
this representation is

**[02:21:59.080] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003000)  
does agree with

**[02:22:01.860] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003001)  
Secretary Giovanni Lopez

**[02:22:04.540] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003002)  
to

**[02:22:05.780] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003003)  
give

**[02:22:07.780] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003004)  
the proposal of

**[02:22:10.000] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003005)  
500 million pesos

**[02:22:13.100] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003006)  
for the service contracting program

**[02:22:16.380] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003008)  
Which has a zero fund for the NEP 2027.

**[02:22:20.840] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003009)  
So, wala pong budget, zero.

**[02:22:27.160] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003010)  
And we feel,

**[02:22:29.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003011)  
even Akbayan Representative Perci Cendaña

**[02:22:33.340] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003012)  
reiterated that the service contracting program

**[02:22:37.140] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003013)  
is of valuable help

**[02:22:38.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003014)  
to the constituents.

**[02:22:42.500] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003015)  
So, I move,

**[02:22:46.380] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003016)  
that the SCP program be given a budget of 500 million pesos.

**[02:22:54.500] Committee member/seconding voice** (asr-003017)  
I second.

**[02:22:55.500] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003018)  
I so move.

**[02:22:57.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003019)  
Yes, before we act on the motion,

**[02:22:59.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003020)  
just a little information for our BARSC members.

**[02:23:03.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003021)  
Nung 2026 BICAM,

**[02:23:06.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003022)  
zero din po ito.

**[02:23:08.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003023)  
But our contingent proposed

**[02:23:11.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003024)  
to put or to allocate 1 billion for this,

**[02:23:15.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003025)  
not knowing na magkakaroon ng energy crisis

**[02:23:18.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003026)  
or war in Iran.

**[02:23:20.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003027)  
So, when the war broke out,

**[02:23:22.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003028)  
may pinagugutan kagad ang DOTR,

**[02:23:24.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003029)  
ang executive,

**[02:23:26.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003030)  
para itulong sa ating mga

**[02:23:27.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003031)  
drivers and operators.

**[02:23:30.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003032)  
So,

**[02:23:31.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003033)  
we're surprised to know na zero pa rin

**[02:23:33.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003034)  
ngayong 2027 proposed budget.

**[02:23:37.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003035)  
So, thus,

**[02:23:38.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003036)  
the sponsor is

**[02:23:40.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003037)  
moving to allocate

**[02:23:42.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003038)  
500 million

**[02:23:43.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003039)  
for this project.

**[02:23:45.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003040)  
Very valuable program.

**[02:23:47.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003041)  
So, there's a motion.

**[02:23:49.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003042)  
Duly seconded.

**[02:23:51.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003043)  
Hearing no objections,

**[02:23:53.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003044)  
motion is approved.

**[02:23:55.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003045)

**[02:24:03.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003049)

**[02:24:05.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003050)

**[02:24:07.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003051)

**[02:24:09.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003052)  
So,

**[02:24:11.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003053)  
this representation agrees with

**[02:24:13.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-003054)

**[03:09:18.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-004153)  
in almost all our regions.

**[03:09:20.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-004154)

**[03:09:23.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004155)  
If I may, Mr. Chair, just to add.

**[03:09:26.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004156)

**[03:09:27.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004157)  
D.S. Emano.

**[03:09:28.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004158)  
Yeah.

**[03:09:29.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004159)  
Mr. Chair, the idea is for Mindanao to have its own disaster center.

**[03:09:33.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004160)  
What usually, we too had our own share of calamities, Mr. Chair.

**[03:09:38.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004161)  
And this is what happened.

**[03:09:40.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004162)  
Our relief goods came from Visayas, from Cebu.

**[03:09:43.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004163)  
So, they fly it through C-130.

**[03:09:45.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004164)  
The problem is C-130 can only accommodate 6,000 packs.

**[03:09:49.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004165)  
So, they tried vessel, granting that it was way after the calamity

**[03:09:53.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004166)  
when weather was fine.

**[03:09:55.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004167)  
But the challenge was the boat can only carry 60,000 food packs.

**[03:09:59.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004168)  
We need a minimum of 3 million food packs in stock

**[03:10:04.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004169)  
if the big one comes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:10:07.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004170)  
That's why we need not only the processing,

**[03:10:09.100] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004171)  
but also the distribution center, Mr. Chair.

**[03:10:13.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004172)  
That is very clear, D.S. Emano.

**[03:10:16.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004173)

**[03:21:44.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004336)

**[03:22:07.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004338)

**[03:22:14.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004339)

**[03:22:22.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004340)

**[03:22:29.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004341)

**[03:22:40.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004342)

**[03:22:44.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004343)

**[03:23:07.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004344)

**[03:23:14.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004345)

**[03:23:26.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004346)

**[03:23:28.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004347)

**[03:23:52.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004351)

**[03:24:01.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004352)

**[03:24:11.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004353)

**[03:24:17.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004356)

**[03:31:16.000] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004546)

**[03:31:17.060] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004547)  
At pagdating nga sa documentation,

**[03:31:19.160] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004548)

**[03:31:21.340] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004549)

**[03:31:23.120] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004550)

**[03:31:25.840] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004551)  
So, pag naayos natin

**[03:31:27.600] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004552)

**[03:31:30.620] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004553)

sa mga kababayan natin.

**[03:31:33.880] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004555)  
Especially in the middle-income sector.

**[03:31:36.120] Rep. Brian Raymund S. Yamsuan** (asr-004556)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:31:37.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004557)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[03:31:38.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004558)

**[03:31:40.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004559)

**[03:31:43.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004560)

**[03:31:45.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004561)

**[03:31:47.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004562)

**[03:31:49.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004563)

**[03:31:51.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004564)  
sa mga taxpayers natin.

**[03:31:53.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004565)  
What is due, especially on health program?

**[03:31:58.080] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004566)  
Siguro, second ko

**[03:31:59.900] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004567)  
si Kong Brian,

**[03:32:02.140] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004568)  
Madam Chair,

**[03:32:04.220] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004569)  
na even a middle-income family,

**[03:32:07.980] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004570)

**[03:32:09.060] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004571)  
ng more than one week,

**[03:32:10.300] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004572)

**[03:32:11.480] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004573)  
ubus na savings niyan.

**[03:32:13.220] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004574)  
So, ang talagang pupuntahan niyan,

**[03:32:16.620] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004575)  
yung MAIFIP.

**[03:32:17.780] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004576)  
Alam natin yan sa probinsya.

**[03:32:20.120] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004577)  
Wala nang pili.

**[03:32:21.120] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004578)  
Like ako, wala akong regional hospital.

**[03:32:23.000] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004579)  
So, karamihan dyan na nagtatrabaho sa gobyerno, even in private companies.

**[03:32:30.900] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004580)  
Pagkat tumagal yan sa ospital, hindi na kaya nila.

**[03:32:33.700] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004581)  
Ubus na savings sila. So, kailangan na nilang tulong.

**[03:32:37.320] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-004582)  
Yan, mga middle income, tinutulungan na rin natin, hindi lang indigent.

**[03:32:43.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004583)  
Madam Chair.

**[03:32:46.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004584)

**[03:32:48.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004585)

**[03:34:06.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004600)

2.6.

**[03:35:07.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-004612)  
2.6, yeah.

**[03:35:09.480] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-004613)  
2.6 billion.
